		limit = {
			exists = character:73889
			exists = character:73888
		}
		character:73889 = { save_scope_as = awow_child}
		character:73888 = { save_scope_as = awow_other_mother}
		awow_assign_historic_father_as_other_mother = yes
		clear_saved_scope = awow_child
		clear_saved_scope = awow_other_mother
	}
}
